         (3)ÊTheÊinitialÊversionÊofÊdocumentsÊfiledÊonÊpaperÊorÊaÊdataÊcarrierÊmayÊonlyÊbeÊ

                                              73
Harare Protocol on Patents, Utility Models and Industrial Designs

 destroyedÊafterÊatÊleastÊfiveÊyearsÊfromÊtheÊdateÊtheÊdocumentÊwasÊincorporatedÊinÊtheÊ
 electronicÊfile.Ê
        (4)ÊAnyÊfilesÊshallÊbeÊpreservedÊforÊatÊleastÊfiveÊyearsÊfromÊtheÊendÊofÊtheÊyearÊ
 inÊwhich-
             (a) theÊapplicationÊisÊrefusedÊorÊwithdrawnÊorÊisÊdeemedÊtoÊbeÊwithdrawn;Ê
             (b) theÊpatentÊlapsesÊinÊtheÊlastÊofÊtheÊDesignatedÊStates.


                             CHAPTER 19
               REPRESENTATION; REQUEST FOR CONVERSION


                                        Rule 72
                               Appointment of Representative
         (1)ÊWhereÊaÊrequestÊforÊgrantÊofÊanÊARIPOÊpatentÊcontainsÊmoreÊthanÊoneÊap-
 plicantÊandÊdoesÊnotÊindicateÊaÊcommonÊrepresentative,ÊtheÊfirstÊapplicantÊnamedÊinÊtheÊ
 requestÊshallÊbeÊdeemedÊtoÊbeÊtheÊcommonÊrepresentative.Ê
        (2)ÊWhereÊoneÊofÊtheÊapplicantsÊisÊobligedÊtoÊappointÊaÊrepresentative,ÊtheÊap-
 pointedÊrepresentativeÊshallÊbeÊdeemedÊtheÊcommonÊrepresentative.Ê
         (3)ÊWhereÊtheÊARIPOÊpatentÊapplicationÊisÊtransferredÊtoÊmoreÊthanÊoneÊper-
 son,ÊandÊsuchÊpersonsÊhaveÊnotÊappointedÊaÊcommonÊrepresentative,ÊparagraphsÊ1ÊandÊ
 2ÊshallÊapplyÊmutatisÊmutandis.ÊWhereÊaÊcommonÊrepresentativeÊhasÊnotÊbeenÊidenti-
 fied,ÊtheÊOfficeÊshall,ÊwithinÊ2Êmonths,ÊinviteÊtheÊapplicantÊtoÊappointÊaÊcommonÊrep-
 resentative.Ê
        (4)ÊWhereÊtheÊinvitationÊreferredÊtoÊunderÊparagraphÊ3ÊÊisÊnotÊcompliedÊwith,Ê
 theÊapplicationÊshallÊbeÊdeemedÊwithdrawn.
         (5)Ê WhereÊtheÊapplicantÊ changesÊtheÊappointedÊ representativeÊorÊappointsÊan-
 otherÊrepresentativeÊapartÊfromÊtheÊrepresentativeÊonÊrecordÊtoÊconductÊaÊspecificÊac-
 tionÊwithÊtheÊOffice,ÊsuchÊrepresentativeÊshallÊbeÊallowedÊonlyÊuponÊsubmissionÊofÊaÊ
 powerÊofÊattorneyÊandÊsubjectÊtoÊpaymentÊofÊtheÊprescribedÊfee.
          (6)ÊWhereÊaÊrepresentativeÊhasÊbeenÊappointed,ÊnotificationsÊshallÊbeÊaddressedÊ
 toÊthatÊrepresentative.


                                            Rule 73
                                         Authorizations
        (1)ÊTheÊauthorizationÊofÊtheÊapplicant'sÊrepresentativeÊshallÊbeÊevidencedÊbyÊaÊ
 powerÊ ofÊ attorneyÊ issuedÊ andÊ signedÊ byÊ theÊ applicant,Ê whichÊ shallÊ beÊ filedÊ withinÊ 2Ê
 monthsÊafterÊfilingÊtheÊapplication.
        (2)ÊWhereÊaÊrepresentativeÊfailsÊtoÊsubmitÊtheÊauthorizationÊreferredÊtoÊinÊpara-
 graphÊ1,ÊtheÊOfficeÊshallÊinviteÊtheÊapplicantÊtoÊcomplyÊwithinÊ1Êmonth,ÊfailingÊwhichÊ
 theÊapplicationÊshallÊbeÊdeemedÊwithdrawn.
      (3)Ê TheÊ authorizationÊ mayÊ coverÊ oneÊ orÊ moreÊ ARIPOÊ patentÊ applicationsÊ orÊ
 ARIPOÊpatents.

                                                74
                     Harare Protocol on Patents, Utility Models and Industrial Designs

         (4)ÊWhereÊtheÊauthorizationÊreferredÊtoÊinÊparagraphÊ3ÊcoversÊpriorÊapplication
(s),ÊitÊshallÊbeÊsubjectÊtoÊtheÊpaymentÊofÊtheÊprescribedÊfee.


                                      Rule 74
                Filing and Transmission of the Request for Conversion
        (1)ÊTheÊrequestÊforÊconversionÊreferredÊtoÊinÊSectionÊ53ÊshallÊbeÊfiledÊwithinÊ3Ê
monthsÊ fromÊ theÊ dateÊ ofÊ theÊ refusalÊ byÊ theÊ OfficeÊ andÊ shallÊ specifyÊ theÊ DesignatedÊ
StatesÊinÊwhichÊtheÊprocedureÊforÊtheÊgrantÊofÊaÊnationalÊpatentÊisÊdesired.
        (2)ÊTheÊOfficeÊshallÊtransmitÊtheÊrequestÊtogetherÊwithÊcopiesÊofÊtheÊapplica-
tionÊ andÊ allÊ relevantÊ documentsÊ toÊ theÊ industrialÊ propertyÊ officesÊ ofÊ theÊ DesignatedÊ
States.


                              CHAPTER 20
                   INTERNATIONAL APPLICATIONS UNDER
                  THE PATENT COOPERATION TREATY (PCT)


                                         Rule 75
                                ARIPO as a Receiving Office
        (1)ÊTheÊOfficeÊshallÊbeÊcompetentÊtoÊactÊasÊaÊreceivingÊOfficeÊwithinÊtheÊmean-
ingÊofÊtheÊPatentÊ CooperationÊTreatyÊifÊ theÊapplicantÊisÊaÊresidentÊorÊ aÊnationalÊofÊaÊ
ContractingÊStateÊtoÊthisÊProtocolÊandÊtheÊPatentÊCooperationÊTreaty.ÊIfÊtheÊapplicantÊ
choosesÊ theÊ OfficeÊ asÊ aÊ receivingÊ Office,Ê theÊ internationalÊ applicationÊ shallÊ beÊ filedÊ
directlyÊwithÊtheÊOffice.Ê
        (2)ÊWhereÊtheÊOfficeÊactsÊasÊaÊreceivingÊOfficeÊunderÊtheÊPatentÊCooperationÊ
Treaty,ÊtheÊinternationalÊapplicationÊshallÊbeÊfiledÊinÊEnglish.Ê
        (3)ÊWhereÊanÊinternationalÊapplicationÊisÊfiledÊwithÊtheÊOfficeÊasÊreceivingÊOf-
ficeÊunderÊSectionÊ57,ÊtheÊtransmittalÊfeeÊreferredÊtoÊinÊRuleÊ14ÊofÊtheÊRegulationsÊun-
derÊtheÊPatentÊCooperationÊTreatyÊshallÊbeÊpaidÊwithinÊ1ÊmonthÊfromÊtheÊfilingÊofÊtheÊ
application.
       (4)ÊTheÊapplicantÊmayÊbeÊrepresentedÊbyÊanÊattorney,ÊagentÊorÊlegalÊpractition-
erÊwhoÊhasÊtheÊrightÊtoÊrepresentÊapplicantsÊbeforeÊtheÊindustrialÊpropertyÊOfficeÊofÊaÊ
ContractingÊState,ÊwhichÊisÊalsoÊboundÊbyÊtheÊPatentÊCooperationÊTreaty.


                                       Rule 76
                      ARIPO as a Designated or Elected Office;
                Requirements for Entry into the ARIPO Regional Phase
        (1)Ê InÊ respectÊ ofÊ anÊ internationalÊ applicationÊ underÊ SectionÊ 58,Ê theÊ applicantÊ
shallÊperformÊtheÊfollowingÊactsÊwithinÊ31ÊmonthsÊfromÊtheÊdateÊofÊfilingÊofÊtheÊappli-
cationÊor,ÊwhereÊpriorityÊhasÊbeenÊclaimed,ÊfromÊtheÊpriorityÊdate-
            (a) furnishÊ anÊ EnglishÊ translationÊ ofÊ theÊ internationalÊ applicationÊ toÊ theÊ
                OfficeÊ ifÊ theÊ internationalÊ applicationÊ wasÊ publishedÊ inÊ aÊ languageÊ
                otherÊthanÊEnglish;Ê

                                               75
Harare Protocol on Patents, Utility Models and Industrial Designs

             (b) payÊ theÊ followingÊ fees,Ê asÊ prescribedÊ inÊ theÊ ScheduleÊ ofÊ Fees,Ê toÊ theÊ
                 Office-
                 (i) theÊapplicationÊfee;
                 (ii) theÊdesignationÊfeeÊperÊcountryÊdesignated;Êand
                 (iii) subjectÊtoÊSectionÊ56(3)ÊtheÊannualÊmaintenanceÊfeesÊwhichÊhaveÊ
                       becomeÊdue;
             (c) whereÊtheÊapplicant’sÊordinaryÊresidenceÊorÊprincipalÊplaceÊofÊbusinessÊ
                 isÊnotÊinÊaÊContractingÊState,ÊwhichÊisÊalsoÊboundÊbyÊtheÊPatentÊCoop-
                 erationÊTreaty,ÊappointÊasÊhisÊrepresentativeÊanÊattorney,ÊagentÊorÊlegalÊ
                 practitionerÊwhoÊhasÊtheÊrightÊtoÊrepresentÊapplicantsÊbeforeÊtheÊindus-
                 trialÊpropertyÊofficeÊofÊsuchÊaÊContractingÊState;
             (d) specifyÊtheÊapplicationÊdocuments,ÊasÊoriginallyÊfiledÊorÊasÊamended,Ê
                 onÊwhichÊtheÊARIPOÊgrantÊprocedureÊisÊtoÊbeÊbased;Êand
             (e) file,ÊwhereÊapplicable,ÊaÊcertificateÊofÊexhibitionÊreferredÊtoÊinÊRuleÊ40.
        (2)ÊInÊrespectÊofÊanÊinternationalÊapplication,ÊtheÊapplicantÊshallÊfileÊtheÊrequestÊ
 forÊexaminationÊasÊprovidedÊforÊinÊSectionÊ45(1)ÊandÊRuleÊ44.
         (3)ÊForÊtheÊpurposeÊofÊRuleÊ47(5),ÊanÊinternationalÊsearchÊreportÊestablishedÊforÊ
 anÊinternationalÊapplicationÊforÊwhichÊtheÊOfficeÊactsÊasÊdesignatedÊOfficeÊunderÊArti-
 cleÊ2(xiii)ÊofÊtheÊPatentÊCooperationÊTreatyÊshallÊbeÊtakenÊintoÊconsideration.
         (4)Ê ForÊ theÊ purposeÊ ofÊ RuleÊ 47(5),Ê anÊ internationalÊ preliminaryÊ examinationÊ
 reportÊestablishedÊforÊanÊinternationalÊapplicationÊforÊwhichÊtheÊOfficeÊactsÊasÊelectedÊ
 OfficeÊunderÊArticleÊ2(xiv)ÊofÊtheÊPatentÊCooperationÊTreatyÊshallÊbeÊtakenÊintoÊcon-
 sideration.
         (5)ÊWhereÊtheÊtranslationÊreferredÊtoÊinÊparagraphÊ1(a),ÊorÊtheÊrequestÊforÊex-
 aminationÊreferredÊtoÊinÊparagraphÊ2ÊisÊnotÊfiledÊinÊdueÊtime,ÊorÊwhereÊtheÊfilingÊfeesÊ
 referredÊtoÊinÊparagraphÊ1(b)ÊareÊnotÊpaidÊinÊdueÊtime,ÊtheÊARIPOÊpatentÊapplicationÊ
 shallÊbeÊdeemedÊtoÊbeÊwithdrawn.


                                 CHAPTER 21
                        REGULATION FOR UTILITY MODEL

                                          Rule 77
                      Applicability of Regulations Relating to Patent
        TheÊRulesÊrelatingÊtoÊpatentÊapplications,ÊparticularlyÊRulesÊ1ÊtoÊ43,Ê45ÊtoÊ49,Ê
 andÊ51ÊtoÊ74,ÊshallÊapplyÊmutatisÊmutandisÊtoÊutilityÊmodelsÊorÊutilityÊmodelÊapplica-
 tions.

                                         Rule 78
                                Examination as to Substance
         (1)Ê ForÊpurposesÊofÊtheÊ examinationÊunderÊSectionÊ63(1)Ê ofÊtheÊProtocol,ÊtheÊ
 OfficeÊmayÊtransmitÊtheÊapplicationÊtogetherÊwithÊallÊrelevantÊdocumentsÊtoÊanÊauthor-
 ityÊspecifiedÊinÊtheÊAdministrativeÊInstructions.
         (2)Ê TheÊ RulesÊ relatingÊ toÊ patentÊ applications,Ê particularlyÊ RuleÊ 50(5)Ê andÊ (6)Ê
 shallÊapplyÊmutatis mutandis.

                                                76
                      Harare Protocol on Patents, Utility Models and Industrial Designs

                               CHAPTER 22
                     REGULATION FOR INDUSTRIAL DESIGN


                                         Rule 79
                     Applicability of Regulations Relating to Patents
         TheÊRulesÊrelatingÊtoÊpatentÊapplications,ÊparticularlyÊRulesÊ1ÊtoÊ12,Ê21,Ê23ÊtoÊ
25,Ê28,Ê32ÊtoÊ34,Ê36ÊtoÊ40,Ê42,Ê54,ÊandÊ62ÊtoÊ74,ÊshallÊapplyÊmutatisÊmutandisÊtoÊindus-
trialÊdesignsÊorÊapplicationsÊthereunder,ÊasÊtheÊcaseÊmayÊbe.


                                           Rule 80
                                          Filing Date
        (1)ÊTheÊOfficeÊshallÊaccordÊasÊtheÊfilingÊdateÊtheÊdateÊonÊwhichÊtheÊapplicationÊ
wasÊ receivedÊ byÊ theÊ OfficeÊ orÊ theÊ industrialÊ propertyÊ officeÊ ofÊ theÊ ContractingÊ StateÊ
withÊwhichÊtheÊapplicationÊwasÊfiled,ÊprovidedÊthatÊtheÊapplicationÊfulfilsÊtheÊrequire-
mentsÊofÊSectionÊ69(1)(a)ÊandÊ(b).
        (2)Ê IfÊonÊtheÊdateÊonÊwhichÊtheÊapplicationÊwasÊreceivedÊbyÊtheÊOfficeÊorÊtheÊ
industrialÊ propertyÊ officeÊ ofÊ theÊ ContractingÊ State,Ê itÊ didÊ notÊ fulfilÊ theÊ requirementsÊ
referredÊtoÊinÊparagraphÊ1,ÊtheÊOfficeÊshallÊaccordÊasÊtheÊfilingÊdateÊtheÊdateÊonÊwhichÊ
theÊapplicationÊfulfilledÊtheÊrequirements.
       (3)ÊWhereÊtheÊapplicationÊdoesÊnotÊcomplyÊwithÊtheÊrequirementsÊreferredÊtoÊinÊ
paragraphÊ 1,Ê theÊ OfficeÊ shallÊ inviteÊ theÊ applicantÊ toÊ complyÊ withÊ theÊ requirementsÊ
withinÊ2Êmonths.
      (4)ÊTheÊOfficeÊshallÊnotifyÊtheÊapplicantÊandÊtheÊIndustrialÊPropertyÊOfficeÊofÊ
eachÊDesignatedÊStateÊofÊtheÊfilingÊdateÊofÊtheÊapplication.


                                          Rule 81
                              Industrial Design Classification
        TheÊOfficeÊshallÊuseÊtheÊclassificationÊadoptedÊbyÊtheÊLocarnoÊAgreementÊes-
tablishingÊanÊInternationalÊClassificationÊforÊIndustrialÊDesignÊ(asÊrevisedÊfromÊtimeÊtoÊ
time)ÊreferredÊtoÊasÊtheÊLocarnoÊclassification.


                                           Rule 82
                                     Statement of Novelty
       ForÊtheÊpurposeÊofÊSectionÊ69(1)(c),ÊtheÊapplicantÊshallÊfurnishÊaÊstatementÊofÊ
noveltyÊwithinÊ2ÊmonthsÊfromÊtheÊfilingÊdate.


                                         Rule 83
                               Payment of Maintenance Fees
       (1)ÊTheÊannualÊmaintenanceÊfeesÊpayableÊunderÊSectionÊ21(1)ÊshallÊbeÊasÊpre-
scribedÊinÊtheÊScheduleÊofÊFees.
       (2)ÊTheÊRulesÊrelatingÊtoÊpatentÊapplications,ÊparticularlyÊRuleÊ36(3)ÊtoÊ(6),Ê(9)Ê
andÊ(10)ÊshallÊapplyÊmutatis mutandis.


                                               77
Harare Protocol on Patents, Utility Models and Industrial Designs

                                         Rule 84
                                   Examination on Filing
        (1)ÊUponÊreceivingÊtheÊapplication,ÊtheÊOfficeÊshallÊexamineÊitÊforÊcomplianceÊ
 withÊtheÊrequirementsÊofÊSectionÊ69(1)(a)ÊandÊ(b).
          (2)ÊWhereÊtheÊOfficeÊfindsÊthatÊtheÊapplicationÊdoesÊnotÊcomplyÊwithÊparagraphÊ
 1,ÊitÊshallÊinviteÊtheÊapplicantÊtoÊcorrectÊtheÊapplicationÊwithinÊaÊperiodÊofÊ2Êmonths.Ê
         (3)ÊIfÊtheÊapplicantÊfailsÊtoÊcomplyÊwithÊtheÊinvitationÊreferredÊtoÊinÊparagraphÊ
 2,ÊtheÊapplicationÊshallÊbeÊdeemedÊwithdrawn.
         (4)Ê WhereÊ theÊ applicationÊ fulfilsÊ theÊ requirementsÊ ofÊ paragraphÊ 1,Ê theÊ OfficeÊ
 shallÊaccordÊtheÊfilingÊdate.Ê
       (5)Ê TheÊ OfficeÊ shallÊnotifyÊtheÊ applicantÊ andÊ theÊ industrialÊpropertyÊ officeÊofÊ
 eachÊDesignatedÊStateÊofÊtheÊfilingÊdateÊofÊtheÊapplication.


                                        Rule 85
                         Examination as to Formal Requirements
         (1)ÊWhereÊanÊARIPOÊindustrialÊdesignÊapplicationÊhasÊbeenÊaccordedÊaÊfilingÊ
 date,ÊtheÊOfficeÊshallÊexamineÊtheÊapplicationÊforÊcomplianceÊwithÊtheÊrequirementsÊofÊ
 SectionsÊ 19(1),Ê 32ÊandÊ69;ÊandÊ RulesÊ4,Ê8(1),Ê8(2),Ê25,Ê28,Ê32,Ê37ÊandÊ38ÊhaveÊbeenÊ
 satisfied.Ê
         (2)ÊTheÊRulesÊrelatingÊtoÊpatentÊapplications,ÊparticularlyÊRuleÊ43(2)ÊandÊ(3)Ê
 shallÊapplyÊmutatisÊmutandis.


                                         Rule 86
                                Examination as to Substance
         (1)ÊForÊtheÊpurposeÊofÊtheÊexaminationÊreferredÊtoÊunderÊSectionÊ72,ÊeachÊDes-
 ignatedÊStateÊmay,ÊwithinÊ4ÊmonthsÊfromÊtheÊreceiptÊofÊtheÊnotificationÊreferredÊtoÊinÊ
 SectionÊ72(2),ÊmakeÊaÊwrittenÊcommunicationÊofÊtheÊoutcomeÊofÊtheÊsubstantiveÊexam-
 inationÊtoÊtheÊOffice.Ê
        (2)ÊTheÊRulesÊrelatingÊtoÊpatentÊapplications,ÊparticularlyÊRuleÊ5(5)ÊtoÊ(7),ÊshallÊ
 applyÊmutatis mutandis.


                                       Rule 87
                   Publication of ARIPO Industrial Design Application
         (1)ÊTheÊOfficeÊshallÊpublishÊaÊnoticeÊofÊanÊapplicationÊtoÊregisterÊanÊindustrialÊ
 designÊinÊtheÊARIPOÊIndustrialÊPropertyÊJournal.Ê
        (2)ÊTheÊpublicationÊshallÊcontainÊtheÊbibliographicÊdataÊandÊreproductionÊofÊtheÊ
 designÊasÊfiled.Ê
         (3)ÊTheÊdesignatedÊContractingÊState(s)ÊshallÊbeÊindicatedÊinÊtheÊpublishedÊap-
 plication.



                                                78
                      Harare Protocol on Patents, Utility Models and Industrial Designs

                                          Rule 88
                           Decision to Register Industrial Design
        (1)ÊWhereÊtheÊOffice,ÊtakingÊintoÊaccountÊtheÊexaminationÊreferredÊtoÊinÊRuleÊ
86,Ê decideÊ toÊ registerÊ anÊ industrialÊ designÊ pursuantÊ toÊ SectionÊ 73,Ê itÊ shallÊ notifyÊ theÊ
applicantÊ togetherÊ withÊ theÊ copyÊ ofÊ theÊ communicationÊ uponÊ whichÊ theÊ decisionÊ isÊ
based.
       (2)ÊTheÊapplicantÊshallÊmakeÊpaymentÊofÊtheÊregistrationÊandÊpublicationÊfeesÊ
withinÊ1ÊmonthÊfromÊtheÊexpirationÊofÊtheÊperiodÊreferredÊtoÊinÊRuleÊ86(1),ÊfailureÊtoÊ
whichÊtheÊapplicationÊshallÊapplicationÊshallÊlapse.


                                          Rule 89
                         Registration of ARIPO Industrial Design
        (1)ÊUponÊ theÊexpiryÊ ofÊ 4ÊmonthsÊprovidedÊ forÊunderÊRuleÊ86,Ê andÊsubjectÊtoÊ
paymentÊofÊtheÊregistrationÊandÊpublicationÊfeeÊinÊaccordanceÊwithÊRuleÊ88(2),ÊtheÊOf-
ficeÊshall-
             (a) registerÊtheÊindustrialÊdesign;
             (b) publishÊinÊtheÊARIPOÊJournalÊaÊreferenceÊtoÊtheÊregistration;
             (c) recordÊtheÊregistrationÊinÊtheÊIndustrialÊDesignÊRegister;
             (d) issueÊtoÊtheÊapplicantÊaÊcertificateÊofÊtheÊregistrationÊofÊtheÊindustrialÊ
                 designÊandÊaÊcopyÊofÊtheÊindustrialÊdesign;Êand
             (e) transmitÊ toÊ eachÊ DesignatedÊ StateÊ forÊ whichÊ theÊ industrialÊ designÊ isÊ
                 registeredÊaÊcopyÊofÊtheÊcertificateÊandÊaÊcopyÊofÊtheÊindustrialÊdesign.


                                          Rule 90
                          Publication of ARIPO Industrial Design
        (1)Ê TheÊ publicationÊ ofÊ aÊ referenceÊ toÊ theÊ registrationÊ inÊ theÊARIPOÊ JournalÊ
shallÊincludeÊtheÊfollowing-
             (a) theÊnumberÊofÊtheÊindustrialÊdesign;
             (b) theÊnameÊandÊaddressÊofÊtheÊownerÊofÊtheÊindustrialÊdesign;
             (c) theÊnameÊandÊaddressÊofÊtheÊoriginatorÊorÊcreator;
             (d) theÊnameÊandÊaddressÊofÊtheÊapplicant'sÊrepresentative,ÊwhereÊapplica-
                 ble;
             (e) theÊfilingÊdateÊofÊtheÊapplication;
             (f) whereÊ priorityÊ hasÊ beenÊ claimedÊ andÊ theÊ claimÊ hasÊ beenÊ accepted,Ê aÊ
                 statementÊofÊthatÊpriority,ÊtheÊpriorityÊdateÊandÊtheÊnameÊofÊtheÊcoun-
                 tryÊorÊcountriesÊinÊwhichÊorÊforÊwhichÊtheÊearlierÊapplicationÊwasÊfiled;
             (g) theÊeffectiveÊdateÊofÊtheÊregistrationÊofÊtheÊindustrialÊdesign;
             (h) theÊtitleÊofÊtheÊindustrialÊdesign;
             (i) theÊmostÊillustrativeÊreproductionÊofÊtheÊindustrialÊdesign;
             (j) theÊsymbolÊofÊtheÊInternationalÊClassification;Êand
             (k) theÊContractingÊStatesÊforÊwhichÊtheÊindustrialÊdesignÊisÊregistered.

                                                79
Harare Protocol on Patents, Utility Models and Industrial Designs

                                           Rule 91
                        Certificate for an ARIPO Industrial Design
          (1)ÊTheÊCertificateÊofÊRegistration,ÊwhichÊshallÊbeÊsignedÊbyÊtheÊDirectorÊGen-
 eralÊofÊtheÊOffice,ÊshallÊcontain
             (a) theÊnumberÊofÊtheÊindustrialÊdesign;
             (b) theÊnameÊandÊaddressÊofÊtheÊownerÊofÊtheÊindustrialÊdesign;
             (c) theÊfilingÊdateÊandÊpriorityÊdate,ÊifÊany,ÊofÊtheÊapplication;
             (d) theÊeffectiveÊdateÊofÊtheÊregistrationÊofÊtheÊindustrialÊdesign;
             (e) theÊtitleÊofÊtheÊindustrialÊdesign;Êand
             (f) theÊContractingÊStatesÊforÊwhichÊindustrialÊdesignÊisÊregistered.


                             CHAPTER 23
               REGULATIONS RELATING TO PAYMENT OF FEES


                                         Rule 92
                  Fees Provided for in the Protocol and the Regulations
         TheÊ feesÊ toÊ beÊ paidÊ toÊ theÊ OfficeÊ underÊ SectionÊ 4,Ê inÊ respectÊ ofÊ anyÊ mattersÊ
 arisingÊ underÊ theÊ ProtocolÊ andÊ theseÊ Regulations,Ê shallÊ beÊ thoseÊ specifiedÊ inÊ theÊ
 ScheduleÊofÊFees,ÊannexedÊtoÊtheseÊRegulationsÊandÊformingÊpartÊofÊtheÊRegulations.


                                           Rule 93
                                     The Due Date for Fees
         FeesÊinÊrespectÊofÊwhichÊtheÊdueÊdateÊisÊnotÊspecifiedÊinÊtheÊProtocolÊandÊtheseÊ
 RegulationsÊÊshallÊbeÊdueÊonÊtheÊdateÊofÊreceiptÊofÊtheÊrequestÊforÊtheÊserviceÊincurringÊ
 theÊfeeÊconcerned.


                                           Rule 94
                                        Payment of Fees
         (1)ÊTheÊfeesÊdueÊtoÊtheÊOfficeÊshallÊbeÊpaidÊinÊU.S.ÊdollarsÊdirectlyÊtoÊtheÊOf-
 fice.
         (2)ÊNotwithstandingÊparagraphÊ1,ÊwhereÊtheÊapplicantÊisÊaÊnationalÊofÊtheÊCon-
 tractingÊStateÊinÊwhichÊtheÊapplicationÊisÊfiled,ÊtheÊindustrialÊpropertyÊofficeÊconcernedÊ
 may-
             (a) acceptÊ paymentÊ ofÊ applicationÊ andÊ designationÊ fees,Ê asÊ prescribedÊ inÊ
                 theÊScheduleÊofÊFees;Êand
             (b) requestÊtheÊ OfficeÊtoÊdebitÊitsÊaccountÊinÊ ARIPOÊwithÊtheÊ amountÊofÊ
                 suchÊfees.
       (3)Ê NotwithstandingÊ paragraphÊ 1,Ê whereÊ theÊ applicantÊ orÊ agentÊ hasÊ aÊ FundsÊ
 HeldÊAccountÊwithÊtheÊOffice,ÊtheÊapplicantÊmayÊmakeÊpaymentÊusingÊtheÊfundsÊheld.
         (4)ÊTheÊARIPOÊOfficeÊshallÊissueÊaÊreceiptÊforÊpaymentÊofÊanyÊprescribedÊfee.


                                                 80
                     Harare Protocol on Patents, Utility Models and Industrial Designs

                                          Rule 95
                             Particulars Concerning Payments
        (1)ÊEveryÊpaymentÊshallÊindicate,ÊwhereÊapplicable,ÊtheÊfollowingÊparticulars-
            (a) applicationÊnumber,ÊreferenceÊnumberÊorÊtitle;
            (b) agentÊorÊapplicantÊname;
            (c) nameÊofÊfee;
            (d) theÊspecificÊamountÊpaid;Êand
            (e) paymentÊmethod.
       (2)ÊWhereÊtheÊpaymentÊisÊtoÊbeÊmadeÊthroughÊbankÊtransfer,ÊtheÊapplicantÊshallÊ
provideÊproofÊofÊpaymentÊwithÊtheÊparticularsÊreferredÊtoÊinÊparagraphÊ1.Ê
       (3)ÊWhereÊ theÊ paymentÊ isÊ toÊ beÊ madeÊ throughÊ fundÊ held,Ê theÊ applicantÊ shallÊ
provideÊ theÊ instructionÊ toÊ theÊ OfficeÊ forÊ paymentÊ withÊ theÊ particularsÊ referredÊ toÊ inÊ
paragraphÊ1.Ê
       (4)ÊWhereÊtheÊpurposeÊofÊ theÊ paymentÊcannotÊimmediatelyÊbeÊestablishedÊbyÊ
theÊOffice,ÊtheÊpaymentÊshallÊbeÊconsideredÊtoÊbeÊmadeÊonÊtheÊdateÊonÊwhichÊtheÊpur-
poseÊofÊtheÊpaymentÊisÊestablished.Ê


                                         Rule 96
                                    The Date of Payment
        (1)ÊWhereÊ theÊ paymentÊ isÊmadeÊ throughÊaÊbank,Ê theÊdateÊonÊ whichÊanyÊ pay-
mentÊshallÊbeÊconsideredÊtoÊhaveÊbeenÊmadeÊtoÊtheÊOfficeÊshallÊbeÊtheÊdateÊonÊwhichÊ
theÊpaidÊamountÊisÊreflectedÊinÊaÊbankÊaccountÊheldÊbyÊtheÊOffice.Ê
        (2)ÊWhereÊpaymentÊisÊmadeÊusingÊfundsÊheld,ÊtheÊdateÊonÊwhichÊanyÊpaymentÊ
shallÊbeÊconsideredÊtoÊhaveÊbeenÊmadeÊtoÊtheÊOfficeÊshallÊbeÊtheÊdateÊonÊwhichÊtheÊin-
structionÊwasÊreceived.
       (3)ÊWhereÊpaymentÊisÊmadeÊthroughÊtheÊContractingÊStatesÊtoÊARIPO,ÊtheÊdateÊ
onÊwhichÊanyÊpaymentÊshallÊbeÊconsideredÊtoÊhaveÊbeenÊmadeÊtoÊtheÊOfficeÊshallÊbeÊ
consideredÊtheÊdateÊtheÊOfficeÊreceivedÊinstructionÊfromÊtheÊContractingÊState.


                                          Rule 97
                             Insufficiency of the Amount Paid
        (1)ÊAÊpaymentÊshallÊbeÊdeemedÊtoÊhaveÊbeenÊmadeÊonlyÊifÊtheÊfullÊamountÊofÊ
theÊfeeÊhasÊbeenÊpaidÊbeforeÊtheÊdueÊdate.Ê
       (2)ÊWhereÊtheÊfeeÊisÊnotÊpaidÊinÊfull,ÊtheÊOfficeÊshallÊissueÊaÊreceiptÊnotifyingÊ
theÊapplicantÊaboutÊtheÊinsufficiency.
       (3)ÊWhereÊtheÊoutstandingÊfeesÊreferredÊtoÊinÊparagraphÊ1ÊareÊnotÊpaidÊonÊtheÊ
dueÊdate,ÊtheÊsurchargeÊprescribedÊunderÊtheÊScheduleÊofÊFeeÊshallÊapply.




                                               81
Harare Protocol on Patents, Utility Models and Industrial Designs

                                 CHAPTER 24
                        APPEAL AND APPEAL PROCEDURE


                                         Rule 98
                       Notice of Appeal and Statement of Grounds
         (1)ÊAnÊpersonÊintendingÊtoÊappealÊtoÊtheÊBoardÊfromÊaÊdecisionÊofÊtheÊOfficeÊ
 shall,ÊwithinÊ90ÊdaysÊofÊtheÊdateÊofÊtheÊdecisionÊofÊtheÊOffice,ÊsubjectÊtoÊtheÊpaymentÊ
 ofÊtheÊprescribedÊfee,ÊfileÊwithÊtheÊSecretaryÊofÊtheÊBoardÊaÊnoticeÊofÊappeal.
         (2)ÊTheÊnoticeÊofÊappealÊshallÊcontain-Ê
             (a) theÊnameÊandÊtheÊaddressÊofÊtheÊappellant;
             (b) theÊdecisionÊimpugned;Êand
             (c) theÊgroundsÊofÊappeal.
         (3)ÊTheÊappellantÊshallÊsendÊaÊcopyÊofÊtheÊnoticeÊofÊappealÊtoÊtheÊDirectorÊGen-
 eral.


                                         Rule 99
                                     Grounds of Appeal
       (1)ÊTheÊgroundsÊofÊappealÊshallÊsetÊoutÊconciselyÊandÊunderÊdistinctÊheadsÊtheÊ
 groundsÊuponÊwhichÊtheÊappellantÊintendsÊtoÊrelyÊatÊtheÊhearingÊofÊtheÊappeal.Ê
        (2)ÊAnÊappealÊwhichÊdoesÊnotÊdiscloseÊclearÊandÊreasonableÊground(s)ÊshallÊnotÊ
 beÊpermitted.
        (3)ÊTheÊappellantÊshallÊnotÊrelyÊonÊanyÊgroundÊofÊappealÊnotÊstatedÊinÊtheÊnoticeÊ
 ofÊappeal.


                                        Rule 100
                       Application for Extension of Time to Appeal
        (1)Ê TheÊ applicantÊ mayÊ requestÊ forÊ anÊ extensionÊ ofÊ timeÊ toÊ appealÊ beforeÊ theÊ
 expiryÊofÊtheÊtimeÊlimitÊreferredÊtoÊinÊRuleÊ98(1),ÊsubjectÊtoÊtheÊpaymentÊ ofÊtheÊpre-
 scribedÊfee.
         (2)ÊTheÊapplicationÊforÊanÊextensionÊofÊtimeÊtoÊappealÊshallÊbeÊsupportedÊbyÊanÊ
 affidavitÊsettingÊoutÊtheÊreasonsÊforÊtheÊapplication.
        (3)ÊTheÊapplication,ÊaccompaniedÊbyÊsupportingÊdocuments,ÊshallÊbeÊfiledÊwithÊ
 theÊSecretaryÊofÊtheÊBoard,ÊandÊaÊcopyÊshallÊbeÊservedÊonÊtheÊDirectorÊGeneral.


                                           Rule 101
                                           Evidence
         (1)ÊTheÊevidenceÊusedÊonÊappealÊshallÊbeÊtheÊsameÊasÊthatÊusedÊbeforeÊtheÊOf-
 fice,ÊandÊnoÊfreshÊevidenceÊshallÊbeÊgivenÊexceptÊwithÊtheÊleaveÊofÊtheÊBoard.Ê
         (2)ÊTheÊBoardÊmayÊsubpoenaÊanyÊpersonÊtoÊgiveÊexpertÊevidenceÊatÊanyÊstageÊ
 ofÊtheÊappeal.


                                               82
                     Harare Protocol on Patents, Utility Models and Industrial Designs

                                        Rule 102
                                   Withdrawal of Appeal
       (1)ÊAnÊappellantÊmay,ÊatÊanyÊtimeÊbeforeÊtheÊhearing,ÊwithdrawÊanÊappealÊbyÊ
givingÊnoticeÊtoÊtheÊSecretaryÊofÊtheÊBoard.Ê
       (2)Ê UponÊ suchÊ noticeÊ beingÊ given,Ê theÊ appealÊ shallÊ beÊ deemedÊ toÊ haveÊ beenÊ
withdrawn.Ê


                                        Rule 103
                             Frivolous or Vexatious Appeals
       WhereÊitÊappearsÊtoÊtheÊBoardÊthatÊaÊnoticeÊofÊappealÊdisclosesÊgroundsÊofÊap-
pealÊwhichÊareÊfrivolousÊorÊvexatiousÊandÊthatÊtheÊappealÊcanÊbeÊdeterminedÊwithoutÊaÊ
hearing,ÊtheÊBoardÊmay,ÊwithoutÊhearing,ÊdismissÊtheÊappeal.


                                        Rule 104
                             Representation Before the Board
        (1)ÊAnÊappellantÊmayÊbeÊrepresentedÊbeforeÊtheÊBoardÊbyÊanÊattorney,ÊagentÊorÊ
legalÊpractitionerÊwhoÊhasÊtheÊrightÊtoÊrepresentÊapplicantsÊbeforeÊtheÊindustrialÊprop-
ertyÊofficeÊofÊanyÊContractingÊStateÊofÊtheÊHarareÊProtocol.Ê
        (2)ÊWhereÊtheÊappellant'sÊordinaryÊresidenceÊorÊprincipalÊplaceÊofÊbusinessÊisÊ
notÊsituatedÊinÊanyÊContractingÊStateÊofÊtheÊHarareÊProtocolÊtheÊapplicantÊshallÊbeÊrep-
resented.


                                     CHAPTER 25
                                  FINAL PROVISIONS


                                       Rule 105
                  Administrative Instruction; Examination Guidelines
        TheÊ DirectorÊ GeneralÊ shallÊ establishÊ AdministrativeÊ InstructionsÊ andÊ Guide-
linesÊforÊtheÊExaminationÊofÊARIPOÊapplications,ÊwhichÊprovideÊdetailsÊandÊguidanceÊ
regardingÊ theÊ applicationÊ ofÊ theseÊ RegulationsÊ andÊ shallÊ notÊ conflictÊ withÊ theÊ provi-
sionsÊofÊtheÊProtocolÊandÊtheseÊRegulations.


                                     Rule 106
                              Amendment to the Regulation
       (1)ÊTheseÊRegulationsÊmayÊbeÊamendedÊatÊtheÊinstanceÊofÊtheÊARIPOÊDirectorÊ
GeneralÊ orÊ anyÊ memberÊ ofÊ theÊ ContractingÊ StateÊ duringÊ theÊ AdministrativeÊ CouncilÊ
session.
        (2)ÊTheÊadoptionÊofÊtheÊamendmentsÊtheseÊregulationsÊshallÊbeÊbyÊaÊsingleÊma-
jorityÊofÊtheÊContractingÊStates.
       (3)ÊTheÊAdministrativeÊCouncilÊshallÊdetermineÊtheÊdateÊofÊentryÊintoÊforceÊofÊ
theÊamendments.

VI.Ê DECLARATIONÊ FORÊ THEÊ PURPOSEÊ OFÊ OBTAININGÊ AÊ SAMPLEÊ OFÊ DEPOSITEDÊ
     BIOLOGICALÊMATERIAL
     The undersigned, identified in the request and hereunder as the certified party, undertakes
     vis-à-vis the applicant for or proprietor of the patent referred to in Part I notÊtoÊmakeÊtheÊbio-
     logicalÊmaterialÊidentifiedÊinÊPartÊIIIÊorÊanyÊbiologicalÊmaterialÊderivedÊtherefromÊavailableÊtoÊanyÊ
     thirdÊparty,ÊandÊtoÊuseÊthatÊmaterialÊforÊexperimentalÊpurposesÊonly,ÊuntilÊsuchÊtimeÊasÊtheÊpatentÊ
     applicationÊ identifiedÊ inÊ PartÊ IÊ isÊ refused,Ê withdrawnÊ orÊ deemedÊ toÊ beÊ withdrawn,Ê orÊ untilÊ theÊ
     ARIPOÊpatentÊgrantedÊbasedÊonÊthatÊapplicationÊhasÊexpiredÊinÊallÊtheÊdesignatedÊstates.
     Where the sample of the biological material identified in Part III is to be made available to
     an expert only, theÊaboveÊundertakingÊappliesÊuntilÊtheÊdateÊonÊwhichÊtheÊARIPOÊpatentÊgrantedÊ
     basedÊonÊtheÊpatentÊapplicationÊidentifiedÊinÊPartÊIÊexpiresÊinÊallÊtheÊdesignatedÊStatesÊorÊwhereÊtheÊ
     applicationÊ isÊ refused,Ê withdrawnÊorÊ deemedÊ toÊ beÊ withdrawn,Ê forÊ twentyÊ yearsÊ fromÊ theÊ dateÊ ofÊ
     filingÊofÊtheÊapplication.Ê
